13. BỔ SUNG FOREX TECHNICAL ANALYSIS FEATURE GOVERNANCE v0.2 — 06/11/2026
CANONICAL DETAILED REGISTER: 01_FOREX_MIGRATION_REGISTER §10. Đoạn này là hợp đồng diễn giải vào lõi, không mặc nhiên bật 100% candidate indicators.
13.1 FEATURE TIERS
TIER CORE_PRICE_STRUCTURE: Dow multi-TF; causal pivots HH/HL/LH/LL/BOS/CHoCH; S/R, range, breakout/retest; closed candlestick evidence; ATR normalisation; quote validation.
TIER CORE_CONTEXT_AND_FEASIBILITY: source calendar/DST, session highs/lows and prior day/week, price-side BID/ASK, dynamic spread/ATR, available news context and correlation/currency exposures as suitable.
TIER CANDIDATE_FILTERS: EMA20/50, RSI14 or ADX14, MACD/ROC, Bollinger/Keltner, Donchian, ADR, regime/noise proxies; roles must be distinct and independently versioned; no mandatory consensus.
TIER CONDITIONAL_SOURCE: tick-volume-derived RVOL/OBV/MFI/VSA (explicit provider proxy) and institutional/venue executed-volume derivatives; without legitimate matching volume source => UNVERIFIED/NOT_APPLICABLE per required-vs-optional contract, never turn invalid source into verified signal.
TIER RESEARCH_ONLY: Wyckoff inferred institutional volume claims, subjective Elliott/Harmonics, unquantified ICT/SMC, provider-unbacked Footprint/Delta/DOM, untuned ML and COT/CME context without access/rights. Monte Carlo is risk/simulation utility, not technical entry signal.
13.2 FEATURE CONTRACTS
Each feature must state: feature_id/code_version; hypothesis and exact computation; data inputs and quote price_side; bar/calendar source; as_of cutoff and first_known; OHLC volume semantics; lookback/warmup/session region; feature scope (zone, regime, trigger, risk, display); required vs optional; degradation when source absent; indicator-family overlap; experiment_manifest/ablation evidence and validity regime.
Causality: no future pivot/right bars, weekly COT release, indicator recalculation or subsequent source corrections before their first-known time. Daily/weekly candle boundaries must be provider-defined and DST-aware.
Quantity: nominal ATR/EMA/RSI parameter seeds are research candidates, not Forex-approved thresholds. No live/confirmed signal eligibility from arbitrary thresholds.
13.3 RECOMMENDED FIRST TESTABLE BASELINE
Structure first: D1/H4/H1 market structure and M15 trigger; conditional M5 verified micro confirmation; previous day/session high-low. Test EMA20/50 for trend role, ATR14 for scale, ADX14 OR RSI14 for supplementary regime/momentum. Add news/session and spread-as-ATR trade safety independently. Compare simple structure-only against addition of each indicator, then multiple combined using strict walk-forward and after-cost metrics. Don't build giant mandatory indicator stack.
13.4 QUALITY OF A TECHNICAL SIGNAL
A signal may be setup-detected but trade-ineligible if spread, bid-ask, macro event, low-liquidity period, margin or source quality blocks execution. Separate detecting a good technical pattern from recommending an executable trade.
13.5 ACCEPTANCE
FX-TA-01..FX-TA-10 are TEST-ID PLACEHOLDERS under 06 §5 REQ-FX-005; 01 §10 is the design decision record (there is no 01 §10.6). Detailed executable testcase definitions and evidence remain UNREGISTERED/NOT_RUN; source validation, broker costs, OOS and ablation still OPEN. User has requested list of KEEP/ADAPT/REMOVE/ADD throughout implementation; record every decision/change evidence in 01 §10.

16. FOREX DIRECTIONAL SETUP TAXONOMY v0.3 — EXTENSIBLE RULE REGISTRY
06/11/2026. User approved new SHORT zones FX-S9 and FX-S10 in DESIGN SCOPE only; earlier pending labels in §15 are superseded. Do not make them trade-eligible until concrete formula/fixtures and validated bid-ask data.
CURRENT CATALOG LABELS=18 (8 B legacy +8 S legacy +2 FX-S). ENTRY CANDIDATE IDs=15 (7 LONG, 8 SHORT), B7 watch-only=1, S3/S8 protective exit-only=2. Rule registry/coverage/audit iteration MUST be versioned dynamic; never hard-code 16 zones/8 S labels for the FOREX project.
Core analysis has eight family mechanisms for comparison: REVERSAL_MAJOR, TREND_PULLBACK, RANGE_REJECTION, FIRST_HL_LH, BREAK_RETEST, COMPRESSION_BREAK, MOMENTUM_BREAK, FAILED_BREAK_LIQUIDITY_SWEEP; each can have LONG/SHORT directional variant, but these 16 conceptual cells aren't '16 validated trades'. Overlap sharing origin_event_id emits one deduped opportunity. B3 long failed-breakdown variant within range is candidate; outside range FX-B9 remains research proposed, NOT added.
FUTURE decisions can MERGE/SPLIT/RETIRE/ADD only with exact causal predicate distinction, outcome data, standalone source availability, net OOS R and false-alert/lead-time improvement; no numeric symmetry quota. Session levels, Carry/Value/FX Fixing, currency strength, news filters may be strategies/variants/context and should not arbitrarily inflate zone counts.
FX-S9 SHORT compression breakdown spec: H1/H4 bearish context, causal lower compression boundary, M15 verified close break, H1/H4 confirmation, no VSA hard gate, bidask/ADR/spread/risk invalidation; overlaps S7/FX-S10. FX-S10 SHORT momentum breakdown spec: causal support, M15 close breakdown, H1/H4 confirmed extension, ADR/spread/news filter, no equity benchmark/RVOL mandatory; overlaps FX-S9/S7. Full manifest in Forex 03 §I.
Trade simulator caveat: actual spot FX has separate value-date settlement conventions, often T+2 for many currency pairs, not stock ownership T+2; retail rolling spot/CFD margin+rollover differs. Instrument-kind contract must distinguish spot/reference/CFD; no blanket 'Forex has no T+2'.

User approved retention of four main signal-display states PER ENTRY-ELIGIBLE DIRECTION, with separate protective/warning semantics for watch-only/exit-only zones. DESIGN_SCOPE_APPROVED, NOT_ENGINEERING_OR_BACKTEST_PASSED.
Same conceptual signal phases for 15 entry candidate IDs: B1–B6,B8 (LONG 7); S1,S2,S4,S5,S6,S7,FX-S9,FX-S10 (SHORT 8). These 15 are DESIGN CANDIDATES, not empirically eligible trade recommendations.
Formal model keeps orthogonal dimensions (a) direction LONG/SHORT, (b) candidate phase prewatch/approaching/early/confirmed, (c) action ENTRY_LONG/ENTRY_SHORT/EXIT_LONG/EXIT_SHORT/WATCH_ONLY, (d) execution eligibility, (e) data/source quality and risk gating. GUI labels are a rendering of facts, not an order and not enough to create action eligible status. No universal assumption that numeric/rank priority is strictly chronological or that every candidate transits through all four states; transitions can skip with causal evidence. Phase names alone do not imply equal criteria for all detector families; each rule's R0/RC/RE/RF manifests apply.
Meaningful guard: CHỜ ... SỚM = structurally prewatch ahead of eligible entry zone with defined proximity conditions; CHỜ ... = WATCH zone with required context; ... SỚM = conditional early evidence tied to closed verified lower-timeframe trigger and usable stop candidate, but not declared higher-timeframe confirmation; ... XÁC NHẬN = all rule-defined confirmed predicates, appropriate closed source candles, invalidation, entry/SL/TP, net after-cost risk feasibility and audits satisfied. Numeric thresholds, timing and exact grade boundaries remain PENDING calibration.
B7 = WATCH_ONLY_DEFAULT even if early bullish micro evidence appears; cannot emit MUA SỚM/MUA XÁC NHẬN as trade recommendation until its special countertrend strategy is separately approved and verified. S3/S8 = protective warning/EXIT_LONG_ONLY; may expose WATCH/EARLY/CONFIRMED WARNING progression, never BÁN SỚM/BÁN XÁC NHẬN meaning opening a new SHORT. With no verified LONG position, show warning only; no calculated actual exit size/order.
Side status separate: INVALIDATED, NO_TRADE, UNVERIFIED, ERROR; data missing => UNVERIFIED (not false NOT_DETECTED); technical setup detected but blocked by spread/news/margin/etc => SETUP_DETECTED, TRADE_INELIGIBLE/NO_TRADE. Conflicting long/short for same pair => conflict policy/no double order, preserve evidence. Provisional/open candles cannot produce CONFIRMED; M5 conditional trigger not override D1/H4 confirm; first_known timestamps and no-lookahead; idempotent rescan same closed bar.
Acceptance tests FX-STATE-01..12 SPECIFIED/NOT_RUN: LONG 4 labels, SHORT 4 labels, skipped-state causal transitions, early-vs-confirmed no-lookahead, M5-only false confirmation negative, B7 watch-only, S3/S8 protective-only, absent position warning-only, missing data UNVERIFIED, spread/news blocked NO_TRADE, duplicate bar idempotence, same event dual side conflict.
This decision SUPERCEDES earlier ambiguity in §2 SIGNAL_STATUS enum and §11 UI proposal only to the extent of these four approved display labels; technical enums may keep different names with explicit mapping. No separate user approval to lock Forex visual UI design or trigger parameters.
18. APPROVED PROFESSIONAL ENGINEERING STANDARD — 06/11/2026
USER REQUIREMENT: Forex Trading 1.0 must be engineered as a professional software project comparable in development discipline to Swing Trading 1.8 — not merely prompt analysis or a collection of indicators. Decision ID FX-ENG-CHARTER-20261106; APPROVED_POLICY_DESIGN, not code/release evidence.
CANONICAL CHARTER: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoWo_nm-Udwl8jBne_rhbhC6kc-kUbUKZguDgdjyBdg/edit
MANDATORY PRINCIPLES: independent private versioned GitHub code repository planned, separate Drive source-of-truth specs and Handoff; typed modular Python contracts/adapters/feature/structure/zone/state/risk/audit/cache/report; common deterministic domain library for live analysis and historical Backtest; immutable source revisions/as-of/first-known; fail-closed for required data/quote/calendar/risk; meaningful unit, integration, contract, regression, negative, replay, quote-side, DST and OOS tests; PR/CI review, test evidence and versioned decisions; selective incremental caching and provenance; no auto broker trading.
RELEASE STAGES are independently tracked: DOCS APPROVED != CODE CI PASS != SOURCE QUALIFIED != DETECTOR TESTS PASS != HISTORICAL BACKTEST != OOS != PAPER != LIVE. Do not inherit equity project Python CI results or tests as Forex proof. Dynamic Forex rule registry currently 18 catalog labels/15 entry design candidates, rule-specific four display states, zero executable validated detectors.
INITIAL WORK: repo/CI bootstrap after repository and access decision, qualify Forex historical feed/rights/quote-side/calendar, start B2 LONG + S2 SHORT typed predicates and fixtures, replay and realistic spread/swap simulator, first historical Backtest once one detector/source qualifies, extend to remaining catalog and then OOS. Do not wait for all rules to begin measuring one rule. No unapproved production broker actions/deploy.
STATUS: Engineering charter is written on Drive, implementation not initiated from this policy alone. This section supersedes generic statements that the project may be built through ad hoc chat-only analysis.
19. INDEPENDENT TECHNICAL CORE AUDIT FINDINGS — GAP REGISTER, NOT APPROVED EXECUTABLE RULES
06/11/2026. Latest audit found gaps in otherwise coherent modular architecture. This §19 documents missing contracts; it does NOT approve thresholds, add trade-entry zones or claim tests.
GAP-CORE-01 P0 EXIT_SHORT PROTECTION: Step 04 Position & Exit Protection is direction-neutral in words but lacks specific SHORT position risk lifecycle, stop/cover trigger side, trailing stops, partial close/margin/rollover and sequence/position verification fixtures. S3/S8 currently protect LONG only; B7 WATCH_ONLY is not automatically an EXIT_SHORT order. Separate protection from 18 zone catalog (no extra entry-zone count).
GAP-CORE-02 P0 EXECUTION BAR PATH: Define broker-specific quote-side activation and fill of SL/TP/stop/limit, simultaneous SL+TP inside candle, spread gap, partial fill, swap rollover, margin stopout, inverse/base/quote/account conversions, and staleness/first-known; never choose optimistic fills by assumption.
GAP-CORE-03 P0 FOUR-STATE FORMULA: Define deterministic predicates/thresholds and evidence per detector for prewatch/wait/early/confirmed; clarify technical_pattern_phase separately from TRADE_ELIGIBILITY and from REPORT_DISPLAY_STATE; expiration/rearm, skip, conflict, invalidation, replay and repeated-bar protection must be tested. Keep user-approved Vietnamese labels and B7/S3/S8 exceptions.
GAP-CORE-04 P0 RULE TESTABILITY: 16 legacy and two new detector manifests remain qualitative. Pin feature_id, temporal event/as-of semantics, quote side, prerequisite source quality, normalized ATR units, lookback/warmup, invalidation and golden/negative source fixtures before any actionable flag.
GAP-CORE-05 P1 REGISTRY/CACHE: Versioned contract/event IDs, deterministic dedup and overlap priority, L1–L5 invalidation/restore, full vs incremental parity, scanner concurrency and bounded resource usage need executable specifications and benchmarks.
GAP-CORE-06 P0 DATA EVIDENCE: Instrument/broker/product, permission, historical BID/ASK (not MID only), spread/slippage and news/calendar validation; no real market backtest with unspecified provider.
GAP-CORE-07 P1 NONFUNCTIONAL: API/error reason codes, observability/SLO, UI data DTO/UAT, retention/security/backup and operational release controls not validated.
RISK-OF-CONFUSION FIXED: §2 reference to 16 is now expressly historical; §13.5 incorrect nonexistent Migration Register §10.6 citation corrected. FX-TA-01..10 are TEST-ID PLACEHOLDERS, not runnable tests. §15 preapproval FX-N01/02 is historical; latest §16 FX-S9/FX-S10 design-scope approval prevails.
REFERENCE: QA full project report 06 §10 https://docs.google.com/document/d/19CGNHVw1QqnTifmtf1Xu5bst0IAbEWLhQlgOoVzfYZg/edit
CURRENT STATUS: GAPS_OPEN, no Forex executable code/Backtest/OOS. Original Swing Trading 1.8 unchanged.
20. EARLY ENTRY + TARGET ZONE + TIMELY EXIT REVIEW — NON-NORMATIVE RFC (06/11/2026)
This section summarizes the completed scope audit only and is NOT an approved numeric/executable contract. Full research record in Forex 07 https://docs.google.com/document/d/1abvr4zk_jz1bbMMs8tWpJHfu3JDum6PfmiMp4oefBKA/edit . Do not change current §17 four-state semantics or 18-label catalog on this basis.
GAP 1: Entry watch/early confirmation needs explicit first-known, closed-candle trigger latency and M15 vs conditional M5 experimental branch; an already known HTF zone can support an early signal without waiting for a NEW H1/H4 close, but cannot be falsely confirmed from open bars.
GAP 2: Introduce candidate EntryZone, Long MAX_ENTRY / Short MIN_ENTRY, status MISSED/LATE/NO_ROOM/SPREAD_BLOCKED, and dynamic after-cost remaining target room; no entry if target is already near or net R/R infeasible. Never move TP away solely to make R/R attractive.
GAP 3: Separate causal take-profit zone service with TP1 nearest viable opposing structure and optional TP2 next structure. Use previously known swing/pivot, range, session/PDH/PDL/weekly high-low, ADR capacity and round-number level context. TP price side: LONG closes BID, SHORT covers ASK. Stop/target fill is not guaranteed by mere candle touch.
GAP 4: Exit manager must independently protect both verified LONG and SHORT positions via invalidation, target, structural stop/trailing, time/no-follow-through and broker/news/margin risk. S3/S8 are EXIT_LONG warnings only; B7 is WATCH_ONLY, cannot substitute automatic EXIT_SHORT action.
GAP 5: Track first-known watch/early/confirmation timestamps, first tradable quote, entry fill and exit/TP touch-vs-fill; compare M15 and M5 conditional time benefit vs early false alerts and net-after-cost expectancy. Track MAE/MFE/giveback and after-cost OOS with identical replay/real-time rules.
RECOMMENDED FEATURE IDS, NOT YET APPROVED FOR IMPLEMENTATION: RFX-ENTRY-01/02, RFX-TP-01, RFX-EXIT-01, RFX-EXEC-01, RFX-METRIC-01. All exact formulas/thresholds, broker quotes, real datasets, tests and backtest remain open/NOT_RUN. This review does not authorize live trading.
21. CANONICAL EARLY SIGNAL / NO-CHASE / TP / EXIT FUNCTIONAL CONTRACT — DESIGN-SCOPE APPROVED 06/11/2026
APPROVAL ID: DEC-FX-010..014 in 01 §16. Detailed normative DESIGN contract is 08 v1.0: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1ojboDkxT2gAyjHKPtv4qMyuskpBUGAu3i81pi-aFdlQ/edit . Prior §20 PROPOSAL status is HISTORICAL and superseded for scope by this §21, NOT for thresholds/code/backtest. Prior approved four display labels §17 unchanged, three non-entry labels unchanged.
METHODS: Four purposeful roles plus target/exit overlay: [STRUCTURE] Dow/HH-HL/market structure, S/R, breakout/retest, failed-break/sweep, session and prior-day levels; [CONTEXT] HTF and EMA20/50 optional, ADX/RSI optional separate role; [EARLY_TRIGGER] closed verified M15 or per-rule allowed closed verified M5 with previously known HTF, no lookahead; [EXECUTION] ATR/ADR room, BID/ASK cost/spread, margin, news/calendar and account exposure; [TP_EXIT] causal opposing zones, invalidation/SL and position-specific exit conditions. No universal indicator consensus; volume-derived features are conditional on legal source and venue validity.
STATE CONTRACT: TECHNICAL_PHASE vs DISPLAY_STATE vs ACTION_INTENT vs DATA_QUALITY vs TRADE_ELIGIBILITY independent fields. PREWATCH/WAIT/EARLY/CONFIRMED needs first-known time and closed-bar predicates. M5 only for early when variant explicitly enabled and does not replace the higher TF confirmation condition. CONFIRMED_TECHNICAL may simultaneously be LATE/NO_TRADE if risk/reward deteriorated. No source+bar gating => UNVERIFIED.
ENTRY CONTRACT: EntryZone [low,high] with known_at, origin ID, trigger and expiry. LONG expected open ASK and max permitted late ASK (MAX_ENTRY); SHORT expected open BID and min permitted late BID (MIN_ENTRY); limits derived from structural stop, economically feasible nearest TP1, dynamic spread/slippage/commission/swap, remaining range and risk policy. NO_TRADE reason when a setup already ran too far or target room exhausted; no retroactive earlier fills.
TP1/TP2 CONTRACT: Zone bounds + causal source/timeframe/first_known & revision, target exit side and reachability; LONG TP executes SELL BID; SHORT TP executes BUY ASK. TP1 nearest admissible opposing structure, optional TP2 next; all stop/limit/touch/vs-fill semantics broker-dependent. Missing/consumed TP => TP_UNVERIFIED or NO_TRADE, not invented mechanical 2R level. Partial vs full TP and dynamic trailing are policy candidates, not fixed allocations.
EXIT CONTRACT: Entry LONG and SELL-to-close LONG are distinct events; Entry SHORT and BUY-to-cover SHORT distinct. EXIT_LONG and EXIT_SHORT support stop, confirmed invalidation, TP touch/fill, trailing, time/no-follow-through and risk emergency; require verified position before computing actual close size/PNL; missing position => warning only. S3/S8 may warn EXIT_LONG, B7 remains watch-only. No new entry zone ID.
EXECUTION COST: per-unit approximate LONG risk=ASKentry−BIDstop, reward=BIDtarget−ASKentry; SHORT risk=ASKstop−BIDentry, reward=BIDentry−ASKtarget; subtract explicit slippage/commission/financing and convert to account currency; never charge spread twice. Pair/broker tick path needed to resolve ambiguous stop+TP in one candle, partial fills, rollover gaps and account mode; don't claim exact broker fills from MID OHLC.
MEASURE: first-known/watch/early/confirmed/delivery/first executable quote/actual fill/TP1/TP2/exit timestamps; false-alert, lead time, late-entry/no-room frequency, favorable move capture, MFE/MAE, giveback, net R after costs and untouched OOS. M15-vs-M5 conditional controlled comparison required. Detailed acceptance designs IDs FX-ENTRY-DES-01..06, FX-TP-DES-01..05, FX-EXIT-DES-01..05, FX-SIG-DES-01..02, FX-MET-DES-01..02 all NOT_IMPLEMENTED/NOT_RUN.
POLICY BOUNDARY: DESIGN_SCOPE_APPROVED; PARAMETER_CALIBRATION_OPEN; PROVIDER/BROKER_LICENSE_OPEN; PYTHON_CODE_NOT_IMPLEMENTED; CI/REAL_BACKTEST/OOS_NOT_RUN; NO_AUTO_TRADING. This normative design text does not make any candidate truly actionable. All 18 zone labels and per-detector 4 display labels preserved.
22. TAKE-PROFIT AND EXIT POLICY — CANONICAL SETUP-SPECIFIC DESIGN v0.6
USER APPROVED completing the missing TP selection/exit control scope. Canonical detailed design: Forex 09 v1.0 https://docs.google.com/document/d/1oangvOAeM8zOiarrQ5BKxTNonBpfNs9Hi5VgUfBuLJo/edit . Prior §21 and 08 §§6–7 remain the broad architecture and action semantics; 09 provides the narrower, more precise authoritative decision policy. Source record DEC-FX-015 in 01 §17; QA REQ-FX-022..024.
TP discovery: causal opposing price zone above planned ASK long entry/below planned BID short entry; first-known source/revision, width/ATR, overlap cluster, broker-relevant quote-side and first material obstacle. Rank nearer opposing obstacles FIRST; do not skip a closer adverse barrier to project attractive distant 2R. If first barrier fails cost-aware minimum net R or no valid source/zone, emit NO_TP_ROOM/TP_UNVERIFIED/NO_TRADE; TP2 optional distinct next known zone, never fabricated.
SETUP SPECIFIC: major reversal first reaction, pullback previous trend extreme, range midpoint versus far boundary as separate variants, HL/LH prior swing, break-retest next external zone, compression/momentum next known opposing structure with strong anti-chase, failed-break midpoint/opposite range. 09 §4 provides directionally mirrored F1–F8 matrix and differences in invalidation and expectancy; no extra zone IDs.
DYNAMIC EXIT: verified-position state only; SELL BID closes LONG and BUY ASK closes SHORT; default protective event families = broker stop/margin, structural invalidation, TP order/touch, favorable causal trail, failed follow-through/time-stop, risk shock. Actual broker fill/order sequence wins over proposed priority. Same-bar SL+TP on OHLC without intrabar path remains AMBIGUOUS_FILL; no profit-first optimistic assumption. Trailing must never widen stops (LONG protective stop only rises, SHORT only falls) and is active only on broker acknowledgment. Any partial TP requires actual fill, residual units and cost/margin reconciliation.
VARIANTS: V1 full TP1; V2 partial TP1+TP2; V3 TP1 then trail; V4 structural/time-managed exit. None selected as universally superior or default without after-cost comparison. Break-even stop NOT automatic at mere TP1 quote touch; must account for bidask/commissions, slippage, order constraints and amendment confirmation.
TESTING AND ACCEPTANCE: TEST-TPX-01..32 design cases (near obstacle, causal first-known, same-bar ambiguity, both sides, partial fills, stop amendment, time/giveback, source/fee/no-lookahead, identical replay) specified in 09 §11, ALL NOT_IMPLEMENTED/NOT_RUN. Exact numeric variants/broker histories still OPEN; no CI/backtest/OOS, and no live order authorization. Design-scope completion != validated operational trading.
23. ENGINEERING CHANGE/RELEASE/ROLLBACK CONTRACT — APPROVED DESIGN SCOPE (06/11/2026)
DEC-FX-017 formalizes professional Forex development and rollback. This §23 is a binding DESIGN-level functional/integration requirement; full technical governance in 10 Standard https://docs.google.com/document/d/19z5m73GJgCu5AYCfio0JJGWHbcPJMwm___djxSq-GtA/edit. 05 Charter governs architecture and engineering controls; 06 QA records acceptance evidence.
INVARIANT: Same pure source-quality/feature/detector/opportunity/TP/Exit domain code is used in scan and historical replay. Adapters are replaceable and versioned. UI, source, risk and broker operations cannot sneak in unreviewed signal predicates. One rule catalog dynamically 18 current labels, 15 entry candidates, and 4 display states each direction, without class duplication. Proper ENTRY_LONG, EXIT_LONG, ENTRY_SHORT, EXIT_SHORT and verified broker position constraints unaffected.
CHANGE CONTRACT: versioned code SHA/dependency lock; typed rule and parameter hashes per pair/product/horizon; source adapter+rights+snapshot revision+first-known+calendar; fee/fill/risk model; schema/cache/report DTO. Every signal, alert, Backtest, audit and experiment pins the full execution profile; params alone can be upgraded/rolled back safely if schema compatible. Never treat a Git commit as sufficient to reproduce a trade.
RELEASE CONTRACT: branch/PR CI gates, change impact, test plan and compatibility before merge; objective paired net-of-cost regression against preregistered previous baseline, positive source rights and untouched OOS for trading-rule changes, no user-unapproved numeric thresholds. Candidate remains inactive if inconclusive/underperforms; ACTIVE and LKG identifiers only after actual approval and observed readiness. Code CI PASS is not signal profitability.
ROLLBACK CONTRACT: explicit compatible rollback modes for code, rule/params, provider/data and append-only corrections, DB/event/cache, UI/report, broker position/accepted orders (future only), docs/decision history. Source revisions, cache invalidation and state migrations must be N/N-1 compatible or fail closed in SAFE_MODE. Old broker fills can never be undone by Git, nor can restored code blindly cancel existing protective stops.
QA: design cases FX-VR-01..20 in 10 §11 are SPECIFIED/NOT_RUN. Future executable Definition of Done requires actual repository+PR+SHA+CI+negative tests+source data/replay/OOS where applicable + recovery drill; all absent today. No Forex code, no releases and no real trading actions authorized.
24. FOREX SMART INCREMENTAL CACHE L1–L5 — APPROVED DESIGN v0.9 (2026-11-07)
APPROVAL DEC-FX-020: Người dùng chấp thuận giữ kiến trúc Smart Cache 5 tầng của Swing Trading nhưng CHỈNH cho Forex, ưu tiên mỗi ~5 phút kiểm tra không tải lại lịch sử còn nguyên giá trị. Không sao chép nguyên các giả định cổ phiếu (VNINDEX, T+2, corporate actions và quy tắc volume chứng khoán); Forex phải có ranh giới theo broker/product/pair, BID/ASK, UTC/session/DST, rollover, spread/first-known và các phiên bản nguồn.
MỤC TIÊU CỨNG: Không tái tải toàn bộ nến lịch sử ở mỗi lần quét 1 hoặc 20 cặp. Khi có quyền dữ liệu và API cho phép, lần đầu bootstrap lịch sử cần thiết có giới hạn; lần sau chỉ truy vấn delta có ranh giới hoặc xác minh change-token/head nhà cung cấp, giữ bản ghi nến cũ nếu không đổi. Không bao giờ tuyên bố server-side delta đã hoạt động nếu adapter thực tế lén tải full history. Không giả định TTL chưa hết đồng nghĩa nguồn chưa sửa dữ liệu.
L1 DATA: Partition fingerprint tối thiểu provider + opaque broker/account PRODUCT reference (không lộ tài khoản) + pair/instrument + BID/ASK/MID + timeframe M1/M5/M15/M30/H1/H4/D1/W1 + source calendar/timezone/aggregation + schema/version/rights revision; closed candles immutable theo source record ID, event/first-known/retrieved UTC, digest và revision. OPEN/PROVISIONAL chỉ overlay tạm, không thay CLOSED đã chốt. Không trộn MID với giá thực thi BID/ASK, không mặc định mọi broker có cùng lịch ngày/đóng nến.
DELTA PLANNER: Ưu tiên bounded-range query từ last qualified closed watermark kèm overlap được phê duyệt để phát hiện late correction; head/change-token có thể tránh tải phần lịch sử cũ CHỈ nếu được kết nối/xác thực từ nguồn thật, còn metadata tự khai chỉ là claim. Nếu nguồn không có bounded endpoint hay không đủ quyền lưu/cache thì BLOCKED/UNVERIFIED; không silently fallback full download. Hệ thống phải xử lý nến đến chậm, APPEND/REVISE/NO_CHANGE/DELETE có tombstone rõ ràng; không suy deletion từ kết quả truy vấn partial. Lịch sử ngoài cửa sổ overlap cần audit định kỳ theo chính sách đã kiểm chứng; không hứa phát hiện tất cả sửa cũ tức thời.
L2 INDICATORS: Lưu checkpoint và fingerprint theo side/TF/source snapshot/calculation code/warmup/parameter version. EMA/ATR/RMA/RSI/MACD/ADX và chỉ báo thích hợp FX ưu tiên tính từ seed/checkpoint hợp lệ trước first dirty timestamp tới hiện tại, rolling chỉ tính cửa sổ phụ thuộc; nếu không đủ seed thì full recompute từ nguồn đã xác minh. Kiểm chứng full vs incremental tương đương, dùng Decimal / tolerance được định nghĩa và trạng thái confirmed/provisional tách riêng. Chưa mặc định mọi indicator đã được triển khai.
L3 STRUCTURE: Pivot có right-bars, swing HL/LH, BOS/CHoCH, vùng S/R/breakout/range là phụ thuộc vào bar/indicator windows và first-known; sửa một M15 có thể làm thay đổi nhiều sự kiện phía sau. Chỉ invalidate và dựng lại các node phụ thuộc explicit, không xóa cache tất cả cặp.
L4 ZONES/SIGNALS: 18 design labels, 15 entry candidates; B7 WATCH, S3/S8 EXIT_LONG_WARNING, FX-B9 research-only; BUY và SELL 4 giai đoạn từng chiều như đã duyệt. Cache key theo rule/param version, nguồn + trạng thái tín hiệu + risk/position context. Tái sử dụng một kết quả không được tăng trạng thái từ early thành confirmed nếu chưa kiểm tra lại tính nhân quả, source/revision và guard.
L5 AUDIT/HISTORY: Mỗi lần phân tích có run_id, source/provider/status, watermark, cache hit/delta bars/corrected bars, indicator invalidation/recompute, bytes/API calls thực đo, 8-step audit và evidence freshness. Report history immutable, supersedes khi có source correction; cache hit không tự chứng minh tám bước đã COMPLETE, không gắn tín hiệu hành động nếu source thiếu.
FINGERPRINT & INVALIDATION: SHA256 canonical serialized identity của nguồn/dữ liệu/quality/calendar/code+deps/indicator/strategy/param/rule/UI/report; dependency DAG theo symbol/TF/side và explicit market-wide tương quan. Thay một nến EURUSD M5 không invalidate GBPUSD H4 nếu không có cạnh phụ thuộc; đổi rule chỉ L4/L5 nếu không sửa feature; đổi UI chỉ L5; source revision từ L1 lan truyền các node tiêu thụ. Cache VALID cần fingerprint + verified quality + freshness + legal right; lỗi/stale => SAFE_MODE / UNVERIFIED, không bỏ qua audit.
PERSISTENCE AND RESTART: Source code và CI ở GitHub, Spec/Handoff ở Google Drive. Giá Forex và indicator snapshots chỉ được lưu tại persistent encrypted/versioned storage khi provider account cụ thể cho phép đọc/lưu/derive/cache/replay/backtest, và khi host, ACL, retention, revocation, checksum/recovery đã được duyệt. Không dùng ChatGPT Python transient làm durable store, không dùng GitHub chứa raw licensed price bars; MOCHI chỉ optional sau duyệt, M15001 không được sử dụng. Dữ liệu cũ phải giữ bền vững qua lần hỏi tiếp theo khi production storage thật được phép, nhưng HIỆN CHƯA CÓ.
ENGINEERING EVIDENCE: PR #11 https://github.com/ngluonghoa/forex-trading-1-0/pull/11 merge SHA caf0df610aeb5dd5b8e9e6ef01f41d5827f29e32 đã thêm Python synthetic pure-core: cache partition, snapshot, bounded planner, immutable delta reconcile, DAG invalidation, EMA append/revise reference, regression tests; GitHub PR Actions 42323752852 và postmerge main Actions 42323816487 Python 3.11/3.12 mỗi bản 224/224 synthetic tests PASS. ĐÂY LÀ FOUNDATION IMPLEMENTED, không phải live L1–L5 cache/persistence/auto polling/hiệu quả tốc độ thật. 02 v0.8 §8 được supersede về chi tiết bởi §24, các business detector và 18 labels giữ nguyên.
ACCEPTANCE CÒN MỞ: (i) có source entitlement retention/backtest & native BID/ASK coverage; (ii) fetch adapter thực tế chỉ lấy delta, đối chiếu đầy đủ/late revisions; (iii) 20-pair cold/warm & 5m poll đo API calls/bytes/latency; (iv) FULL-vs-INCREMENTAL parity EMA/ATR/RSI/MACD/ADX + 18 label states và TP/Exit; (v) crash/restart/rollback/TTL-corruption/rights revoked/metadata + snapshot consistency; (vi) correct source session/DST and clock with no lookahead; (vii) storage/host/security approval. Không thông qua release/backtest/live khi bất kỳ gate bắt buộc nào thiếu.

It detects two danger paths: unchanged claimed remote head despite older candle corrected, and even changed head with correction OUTSIDE the small overlap. Incremental reconciliation cannot discover unseen revision without authoritative vendor event/revision feed or scheduled, permitted deep-history reconciliation. Such cases explicitly classified UNSEEN_HISTORY_CORRECTION in synthetic tests and must remain UNKNOWN/UNVERIFIED for production; no guarantee that recent-overlap alone proves old data unchanged.

Hash chain is LOCAL consistency only: no verified vendor signature, immutable external anchor, genuine network ingest, contract rights or 24/7 custody. assess_synthetic_custody never grants vendor authenticity/retention/backtest rights.

BEGIN CANONICAL 02_FOREX_MASTER_SPEC v0.10 FULL EXTRACTED TEXT
FOREX TRADING 1.0 — MASTER SPEC v0.10 (DESIGN CONTRACT / OPERATIONAL IMPLEMENTATION PENDING)
0. PHẠM VI, THỨ TỰ ƯU TIÊN VÀ GIAO ƯỚC NGUỒN CHUẨN — FOREX TRADING 1.0
CURRENT FUNCTIONAL DESIGN BASELINE v0.10 · 07/11/2026. Đây là Master Spec theo cách tổ chức của Swing Trading: tài liệu này QUY ĐỊNH điều hệ thống phải làm và các điều kiện nghiệm thu, không báo cáo rằng mã nguồn đã tồn tại. 00_MASTER_HANDOFF §0/§22 là nguồn chuẩn trạng thái/NEXT; 01 quyết định; 06 traceability và test evidence. Nếu nội dung cũ mâu thuẫn với các quyết định hiện hành, dùng §23 và 10 cho governance/rollback, §22 rồi §21/§17/§16 cho tín hiệu/TP theo phạm vi được duyệt; giữ các mục cũ để truy vết thay vì lặng lẽ xóa.
0.1. QUY TẮC NGHIỆP VỤ BẤT BIẾN Ở CẤP THIẾT KẾ
- Dự án Forex độc lập với Swing Trading 1.8/CK 2.0/FX 2.0/Scanner; không sao chép quy tắc volume, VNINDEX/T+2 hay hành động bán cổ phiếu nắm giữ thành SHORT Forex.
- Phân tích 2 hướng LONG/SHORT và horizon giao dịch tách riêng; Entry và Exit là 4 action-intents: ENTRY_LONG BUY (ASK), EXIT_LONG SELL (BID), ENTRY_SHORT SELL (BID), EXIT_SHORT BUY (ASK); netting/hedging và position verification là điều kiện bắt buộc trước hành động đóng thực.
- 18 nhãn catalog thiết kế động: 7 ứng viên ENTRY_LONG, 8 ENTRY_SHORT, B7 WATCH_ONLY, S3/S8 EXIT_LONG_WARNING_ONLY; không đồng nhất số nhãn với detector executable. Bốn trạng thái hiển thị trên mỗi hướng là CHỜ MUA SỚM/CHỜ MUA/MUA SỚM/MUA XÁC NHẬN và CHỜ BÁN SỚM/CHỜ BÁN/BÁN SỚM/BÁN XÁC NHẬN; technical phase ≠ dữ liệu hợp lệ ≠ trade eligible.
- Pipeline 8 bước, 8 chủ đề audit, as-of/first-known nhân quả, source-quality hard fail và báo cáo lỗi/NO_TRADE; M15 closed verified là baseline, M5 closed verified chỉ dùng có điều kiện và không giả xác nhận HTF; anti-chase, EntryZone, stop cấu trúc, TP/Exit dựa trên vùng đã biết và net-after-cost.
- Mọi setup phải đối mặt vùng cản đầu tiên: TP1 là vùng đối diện hợp lệ gần nhất nếu còn lợi nhuận sau phí; TP2 là vùng kế tiếp nếu có và khả thi; không bỏ qua cản gần để vẽ TP xa; 4 biến thể quản lý V1 full TP1, V2 partial TP1/TP2, V3 TP1+trail, V4 structural/time. SL không được nới lỏng; chỉ công nhận lệnh khớp bằng chứng broker/quote side; cùng nến chạm SL và TP mà không có intrabar path => AMBIGUOUS_FILL.
- Quyết định NO_TRADE/UNVERIFIED/ERROR được phép và phải giải thích bằng nguồn/điều kiện cụ thể. Không phát hành giao dịch tự động hay công bố kết quả lợi nhuận chưa được đo.
0.2. BẢN ĐỒ HỢP ĐỒNG HIỆN HÀNH
02 §§1–12: kiến trúc pipeline/input-output, data/quality/risk, source/Backtest (chỉ trong phạm vi chưa bị thay thế).

03 §I/J/K/L/M: detector manifests và rule-version compatibility, stage mapping và TP overlay; 08 §§2–5,8–12: action ontology, early signal và giao ước báo cáo; 09 §§1–13: CHÍNH SÁCH CHUYÊN BIỆT QUYẾT ĐỊNH TP1/TP2/SL/TRAIL/EXIT, ưu tiên hơn mô tả tổng quát của 08 §§6–7; 01 §§16–19 ghi phê duyệt và chuẩn quản trị; 05 §§15–17 engineering/document/release governance; 06 §§2/5/18 quản trị yêu cầu→test→evidence; 10 §0–13 là chính sách versioning, PR/CI, LKG, rollback chi tiết.

0.3. TRẠNG THÁI CHẤT LƯỢNG VÀ PHẠM VI CHƯA ĐƯỢC NGHIỆM THU
APPROVED_DESIGN_SCOPE chỉ là đồng ý mô hình và trách nhiệm module. SPECIFIED/PLANNED TEST là bộ test lý thuyết, không phải pytest đã chạy. CODE_IMPLEMENTED chỉ khi có commit/PR/CI/runner bằng chứng Forex; SOURCE_VERIFIED chỉ khi có provider/broker quote + quyền dùng + clock/price side; BACKTEST_RUN chỉ khi có replay, historical entitlement và cost simulator; OOS_PASS/PAPER_PASS/RELEASED phải có run manifest, dữ liệu, thời điểm và log. Không kế thừa PASS của Swing Trading.
Hiện tại: FOREX Python/repo/CI NOT_VERIFIED/NOT_STARTED; dữ liệu licensed BID/ASK BLOCKED_SOURCE; numeric detector/TP/SL/Exit policy parameters PARAM_PENDING; TEST-TPX-01..32 và các test design chưa chạy; không có real Backtest/OOS/paper. Không coi R:R đẹp trên chart là lợi nhuận đã chứng minh. Các lựa chọn tỷ lệ partial TP, minimum net R, N time stop, trailing ATR/giveback và broker order type chờ dữ liệu và duyệt riêng.
0.4. QUY TRÌNH CẬP NHẬT MASTER SPEC ĐỒNG BỘ HANDOFF
Thay đổi nghiệp vụ: tạo DEC-FX ID + approved scope tại 01; sửa đúng mục nguồn chuẩn trong 02/08/09/03, kèm version và điều kiện; cập nhật REQ-FX, test-id, trạng thái chứng cứ tại 06; cập nhật 00 checkpoint và NEXT; đọc lại nhất quán 18 nhãn, 4 trạng thái, EXIT_LONG/EXIT_SHORT, quyền dữ liệu. Thay đổi code tương lai: branch/PR, lint/type/pytest negative tests, commit+SHA+CI, đối chiếu run log; GitHub snapshot của tài liệu phải so khớp nguyên văn với Google Drive khi và chỉ khi đã tạo repo. Không tự giả tạo đồng bộ Drive/GitHub hoặc báo release khi chỉ có bản đặc tả.
LỊCH SỬ TẠO DỰ ÁN · 06/11/2026 · BẢN THIẾT KẾ BAN ĐẦU — XEM §0 CHO HIỆN HÀNH
PHẠM VI Ở THỜI ĐIỂM KHỞI TẠO (LỊCH SỬ): phân tích kỹ thuật Forex, LONG/SHORT, watch/early/confirmed, source-quality audit và Backtest. Trạng thái đặc tả hiện hành đọc §0/§21/§22; tuyệt đối không coi đoạn khởi tạo này là nguồn chuẩn mới nhất.
1. MỤC TIÊU NGHIỆP VỤ VÀ PHẠM VI
Input: một hoặc nhiều cặp tỷ giá, market/as-of timestamp, source/broker assumptions, optional position and account/risk constraints.
Output: cho mỗi cặp hướng LONG và SHORT riêng, vùng chờ/mức kích hoạt/early/confirmed, Entry/SL/TP1/TP2, R:R net of modeled costs, invalidation, main/secondary setup, time horizon, Market/Session/Macro Context, 8-step evidence, 8 audit topics, source-quality, cache stats, NO TRADE/ERROR/INCOMPLETE.
Giới hạn P1: major/cross currency pairs, không tự gộp vàng XAUUSD, bạc, crypto, chỉ số CFDs dưới cùng thị trường Forex; các loại đó đòi instrument class riêng. Không đặt lệnh. Nguồn chỉ được đọc theo quyền truy cập.
Phân biệt INFO_SIGNAL (mô hình phát hiện), TRADE_ELIGIBLE (đủ nguồn/chi phí/risk), ORDER_PLACED (ngoài phạm vi) và HISTORICAL_VALIDATED (kiểm nghiệm thực tế). Cấm gắn nhãn trading-eligible khi thiếu nguồn/clock/stop/costs cần thiết.
2. MÔ HÌNH TRẠNG THÁI — PHẢI TÁCH HAI TRỤC
DIRECTION = LONG | SHORT.
STRATEGY_HORIZON = FAST | SWING (tên, tối thiểu/tối đa holding duration chỉ là dự thảo; không lấy cổ phiếu SHORT 3–10 phiên, SWING 10–60 phiên làm default).
SETUP_PHASE = WATCH_CANDIDATE | APPROACHING | EARLY | CONFIRMED | INVALIDATED.
SIGNAL_STATUS = WAIT_EARLY | WAIT | EARLY_SIGNAL | CONFIRMED_SIGNAL | NO_TRADE | UNVERIFIED | ERROR. Giao diện tiếng Việt có thể hiện CHỜ MUA SỚM / CHỜ MUA / MUA SỚM / MUA XÁC NHẬN và đối xứng bên bán, nhưng không biến nhãn hiển thị thành lệnh được khớp.
POSITION_STATUS = NOT_PROVIDED | FLAT_VERIFIED | OPEN_VERIFIED | STALE | CONFLICTING; đồng thời biết direction, units và margin when provided. Nếu không có position, không được suy ra tài khoản flat.
ZONE_ACTION = ENTRY_LONG | ENTRY_SHORT | EXIT_LONG | EXIT_SHORT | WATCH_ONLY; ZONE_AUXILIARY có thể gắn cảnh báo chống mua đuổi/chốt lời độc lập với lệnh SHORT.
RULE_EVALUATION = DETECTED | NOT_DETECTED | UNVERIFIED | NOT_APPLICABLE (chỉ dùng N/A khi rule thực sự ngoài strategy/instrument scope, có reason). Danh mục 16 vùng là lịch sử kế thừa. Danh mục hiện hành gồm 18 nhãn theo §16 và được quản lý động theo phiên bản; không khóa cứng số lượng.
3. 8 BƯỚC PIPELINE DỰ THẢO — GIỮ CHUẨN MINH BẠCH
01 DATA MANAGER: pair registry, provider/broker scope, source license, timeframe price-side, source revision, L1 cache/source delta.
02 DATA VALIDATOR: timestamp/provider time, timezone/DST, weekend/rollover, bid/ask OHLC, stale/crossed quotes, spread anomalies, missing bar, tick-volume provenance and source history completeness.
03 ANALYSIS ENGINE: trend W1/D1/H4/H1/M30/M15 + conditional M5; structure/supply-demand/ranges, currency strength and cross-pair regime, volatility/session/news; indicators with versioned dependencies.
04 POSITION & EXIT PROTECTION: verified open positions, adverse spread/stop-loss, margin/free margin/stop-out, expected swap/rollover, weekend/gap and news exposure; no assumed active position.
05 ZONE DETECTION: all active catalog rules with separate LONG/SHORT/EXIT applicability, main/secondary zone IDs, watch, early and confirmed evidence.
06 SIGNAL ENGINE: strategy_horizon × direction, Entry/SL/TP1/TP2/max chase, net R:R, pip/lot sizing, correlated currency risk, invalidation and expiry.
07 AUDIT & DECISION: eight independent audits, hard fail, source/price-side/calendar/news/portfolio feasibility, ranking, NO TRADE allowed.
08 REPORT & HISTORY: atomic versioned evidence+snapshot, verified status, stable UI template, no contradictory FULL_REPORT when critical source missing; store event IDs for audit and replay.
Mỗi stage có NOT_STARTED/RUNNING/COMPLETE/FAILED/BLOCKED với timestamp và evidence; cache re-use không đồng nghĩa stage COMPLETE nếu input không được tái xác minh. No 8/8 verified claim from mocked data.
4. HỢP ĐỒNG DỮ LIỆU FX v0.1 — CANDIDATE
InstrumentKey = {pair, base_ccy, quote_ccy, instrument_kind, broker_or_reference_market, precision, pip_size, tick_size, contract_size, quote_currency, account_currency_conversion_spec_version}.
ProviderObservation = {provider_id, source_stream_id, endpoint, raw_snapshot_digest, quote_side, first_known_utc, retrieved_at_utc, license_scope, source_revision, timezone_source_policy, broker_contract_version}.
MarketBarKey = {instrument_version, timeframe, bar_start_utc, bar_end_utc, source_calendar_version, aggregation_policy_version, quote_side}; separate from provider observation identity and revision. BID, ASK and MID are semantically different bar inputs, not silently interchangeable.
Quote = {event_time_utc, first_known_time_utc, bid, ask, source_id, stale_flags, quote_spread, quote_revision}; require bid<=ask and nonnegative prices unless specific type contradicts and has handling. MID=(bid+ask)/2 when both available; MID is a derived reference, not executable. OHLC for BID/ASK should derive from quote streams where possible; snapshot OHLC MID alone is insufficient to certify executable fills.
Bar quality axes independent: lifecycle OPEN/CLOSED; coverage COMPLETE/PARTIAL/SPARSE_UNVERIFIED/UNKNOWN/INVALID; source quality VERIFIED/WARN/UNVERIFIED/BLOCKED. A CLOSED bar with bad source is NOT a confirmed signal.
Time labels: source event timestamp, observation first-known timestamp, scan decision timestamp, candle close cutoff timezone-aware; reject mismatched parsing and ambiguous DST. W1/D1 period policy can be broker/source-specific, especially New York close and Sunday short candles.
Tick volume/quote count: provider-specific proxy only, not aggregate global market volume. Reject default 'no quote = no trade' inference; neither volume=0 nor empty candles implies zero currency market turnover.
Source conflict: if two providers/BID/ASK/origin revisions disagree, mark conflict; never silently merge, even when all digest SHA256 match.
Data retention: raw source logs may contain restricted/licensed content. Record permission before persistence or GitHub snapshot; no secrets/raw market feeds committed.
5. PHÂN TÍCH, CHỈ BÁO VÀ NGỮ CẢNH
Mandatory to evaluate context: multi-TF trend and structure; price action; key levels/ranges; volatility; session context; spread/execution risk; available macro events; pair and portfolio exposure when multiple pairs.
Candidate indicators: EMA/SMA, RSI, MACD, ADX/DMI, ATR, Bollinger/Keltner, ROC, Fibonacci/Pivots; choose only when rule-specific improvement survives validation.
Conditional/research: Wyckoff, VSA, VCP, AVWAP/VWAP, OBV, MFI, RVOL-TOD, order-flow/footprint, ICT/SMC, ML. Data-dependent indicators must use valid proxy or UNVERIFIED; not all required.
Currency Strength must handle base/quote signs and pair coverage; derived dollar strength and EURUSD cannot be treated as independent confirmations if driven by same prices.
MacroEvent: scheduled_release_time and actual_release_first_known_time distinct; no later actual values used to predict past decisions. Event severity/blackout thresholds are candidates, not assumed fixed.
6. DETECTOR MODEL — BASELINE v0.1 LỊCH SỬ; HIỆN HÀNH 18 NHÃN Ở §16
16 baseline B/S from Swing source are evaluated for applicability, not mechanically copy-traded. Candidate rules:
B1 major bottom reversal LONG; B2 trend pullback LONG; B3 range support LONG; B4 first HL LONG; B5 breakout retest LONG; B6 bullish compression LONG; B7 downtrend technical bounce LONG WATCH_ONLY by default; B8 momentum breakout LONG with anti-chase.
S1 major top reversal SHORT; S2 bearish retracement SHORT; S3 top within uptrend LONG_EXIT_ONLY default; S4 range resistance SHORT; S5 first LH SHORT; S6 failed bullish breakout SHORT; S7 breakdown retest SHORT; S8 blow-off exhaustion WARNING/EXIT_ONLY default.
A detector can exist as structure label but not be actionable. Future research may split mirrored LONG/SHORT variants or retire unhelpful labels after documented review, not silently delete source lineage.
Every detector manifest: rule_id, semantic_version, direction, allowed_actions, horizons, required timeframes, optional features, required source quality, phase predicates WATCH/EARLY/CONFIRM, invalidation, stop origin, anti-chase limit, cost/margin gates, test fixtures and reviewer.
Predicate thresholds P01–P17 from stocks MUST NOT be reused as approved Forex parameters. New FX_PARAM_REGISTRY starts SPEC_PENDING_CALIBRATION.
First executable detector should be selected by clear rules and data availability (for example B2 LONG or S2 SHORT), not picked by hypothetical profitability.
7. FOREX RISK/EXECUTION PRINCIPLES
LONG buy enters at ASK, closes at BID; SHORT enters at BID, closes at ASK, subject to broker/product specifics. Stop/TP activation and fill must be simulated with the executable side, not against midpoint only.
Net PnL = direction-aware entry/exit difference × units, converted to account currency using timestamped conversion prices, minus actual/slippage/commission/spread already embedded in bid/ask and financing/swap costs as applicable. Avoid double-counting spread if using bid/ask fills and adding fees.
Risk needs equity/account currency, pip value conversion, stop distance, units/lot increments, margin requirement, maximum leverage, drawdown/stress, weekend gaps and multiple correlated positions. Unknowns -> cannot claim exact position size.
Trailing stop/Stop loss not guaranteed to execute at exact price in gap/illiquid conditions. Model nonfills/partial fills and stop-outs if product supports.
Spread/rollover/news/counterparty are hard risk blockers by per-strategy thresholds to be calibrated, not arbitrarily auto-approved.
8. CACHE / EARLY DETECTION
L1 raw/normalized multi-side quotes/bars, session events, macro, source quality, currency registry and broker contract.
L2 indicators, currency strength, relative/correlation metrics; independent price-side/TF/source-version hashes.
L3 structure/pivots/BOS/CHoCH, range and breakout event history, causal first-known clocks.
L4 zone phases, LONG/SHORT ideas and risk input dependencies; no repeated alert on rescanning same closed bar.
L5 audit, report snapshots, manifest, versioned signal history; immutable old evidence with supersedes.
M15 always analyzed when verified; M5 triggered conditionally by near-zone or risk-protection and not by repeated polling. A provisional M5 can display watch/preview only. Clock-based expiry requires defined calendar/rollover policy; correction replays prior M15 state, not counts as another new bar.
Refresh every ~5 minutes on demand should reuse historical snapshots and recompute only changed windows; server-side delta must be verified rather than promised.
9. BACKTEST & SIGNAL VALIDATION ENGINE
Backtest must use exactly the same detector/risk implementation as analysis engine. Separate ReplayClock, HistoricalMarketDataPort, TradeSimulator, ExperimentManifest, EventScorer, PortfolioSimulator and Metrics; no code paths for live orders.
Layer 1 EVENT METRICS: precision/recall with defined label horizon, false alerts per pair-session, early lead time and lost moves; label outcomes never leak into predictor inputs.
Layer 2 TRADING SIMULATION: bid/ask cost after first-known time, spread variation, commission/financing, margin leverage, account conversion, weekend gaps, news events, unfilled/partial fills, portfolio currency concentration. Net expectancy in R, fill rate, drawdown, profit factor and uncertainty, not win rate alone.
Layer 3 ROBUSTNESS: chronological train/validation/walk-forward/untouched OOS, regime stratification by session/volatility/event, provider sensitivity, multiple comparisons and survivorship/availability. Final untouched OOS is not used to tune.
Start with synthetic correctness harness and one complete executable detector; FIRST REAL historical backtest only with licensed time-appropriate data and complete execution assumptions. Do NOT wait all 16 detectors, but do NOT skip required data gates.
Compare baseline: M30 only vs M15 vs M15+adaptive M5, using same data scope/fee assumptions. H4 enhancement should be tested as independent ablation, not assumed beneficial.
10. TEST / ACCEPTANCE REGISTER — PROPOSED (NOT RUN)
FX-DC-01 bid ask valid/crossed; FX-DC-02 mid only cannot fill; FX-DC-03 clock DST fallback ambiguity; FX-DC-04 New York daily rollover and Sunday bar; FX-DC-05 weekend gap; FX-DC-06 provider discrepancy; FX-DC-07 tick volume missing vs zero; FX-DC-08 source rights and first-known.
FX-SIG-01 causal pivot no look-ahead; FX-SIG-02 repeated M15 bar dedup; FX-SIG-03 source revision replay; FX-SIG-04 LONG/SHORT directions noncollision; FX-SIG-05 S3/S8 EXIT_ONLY; FX-SIG-06 B7 WATCH_ONLY; FX-SIG-07 INCOMPLETE vs valid NO_SIGNAL; FX-SIG-08 8/8 report & audit gates.
FX-EX-01 LONG ask→bid; FX-EX-02 SHORT bid→ask; FX-EX-03 spread widened; FX-EX-04 swap at rollover; FX-EX-05 pip value quote/account currency conversion; FX-EX-06 margin stopout; FX-EX-07 weekend stop gap; FX-EX-08 partial/no-fill after known time.
FX-PORT-01 correlated USD exposure; FX-PORT-02 multi-pair risk cap; FX-PORT-03 currency strength lookahead and data coverage; FX-PORT-04 conflicting LONG/SHORT net exposure.
FX-BT-01 deterministic replay; FX-BT-02 identical rules engine vs backtest; FX-BT-03 chronological holdout; FX-BT-04 cost-aware metrics; FX-BT-05 M30/M15/M5 ablation; FX-BT-06 macro first-known and hindsight trap.
All registered tests remain SPECIFIED/NOT_RUN until committed runnable tests, fixtures, run logs and reviewed evidence exist.
11. UI FOREX PROPOSAL — CHƯA KHÓA
A Header: pair, source, BID/ASK/MID, timestamp/quality, 8-step gate.
B Context: H4/H1/D1, currency strength/correlation, sessions, macro/event risk.
C LONG opportunities by FAST/SWING.
D SHORT opportunities by FAST/SWING.
E Active position protection including margin/swaps/weekend; if none supplied, show NOT_PROVIDED.
F Zone coverage and detector applicability; include EXIT_ONLY/WATCH_ONLY rather than fake sell triggers.
G Eight detailed audits and hard failures.
H Cache/evidence/backtest version status.
I Conclusion: LONG/SHORT/WAIT/NO TRADE, why, next alert trigger, invalidation. No live account execution.
The stock UI-1.0 visual lock remains in stock project only. Forex UI proposal requires its own explicit user approval; don't copy visual design unreviewed.
12. OPEN QUESTIONS REQUIRING VALIDATION BEFORE CODE
Q1 Spot indicative/reference or specific FX CFD/rolling spot broker instrument? This impacts quote/execution/margin/swap contracts.
Q2 Which provider offers legally usable historical bid/ask/M1/M5, event timestamps and real entitlements? We will compare candidates; no assumption of availability.
Q3 FAST and SWING holding horizons, trade direction eligibility, pair list and FX news risk policy?
Q4 Margin/account currency/contract sizes and broker if any; if absent, report risk ranges with assumptions, never exact executable sizing.
Q5 Volume/tick source and whether VSA/RVOL/VWAP can be included as required or optional?
Q6 Historical OOS holdout windows, trade fees/rollover by date and data storage permission?
Q7 Separate GitHub repo/host/backup architecture to approve before runtime build?
Status: DOC_SPEC_DRAFT v0.1 only. No client account connected; no Forex data read as verified, no Backtest run and no broker orders.
13. BỔ SUNG FOREX TECHNICAL ANALYSIS FEATURE GOVERNANCE v0.2 — 06/11/2026
CANONICAL DETAILED REGISTER: 01_FOREX_MIGRATION_REGISTER §10. Đoạn này là hợp đồng diễn giải vào lõi, không mặc nhiên bật 100% candidate indicators.
13.1 FEATURE TIERS
TIER CORE_PRICE_STRUCTURE: Dow multi-TF; causal pivots HH/HL/LH/LL/BOS/CHoCH; S/R, range, breakout/retest; closed candlestick evidence; ATR normalisation; quote validation.
TIER CORE_CONTEXT_AND_FEASIBILITY: source calendar/DST, session highs/lows and prior day/week, price-side BID/ASK, dynamic spread/ATR, available news context and correlation/currency exposures as suitable.
TIER CANDIDATE_FILTERS: EMA20/50, RSI14 or ADX14, MACD/ROC, Bollinger/Keltner, Donchian, ADR, regime/noise proxies; roles must be distinct and independently versioned; no mandatory consensus.
TIER CONDITIONAL_SOURCE: tick-volume-derived RVOL/OBV/MFI/VSA (explicit provider proxy) and institutional/venue executed-volume derivatives; without legitimate matching volume source => UNVERIFIED/NOT_APPLICABLE per required-vs-optional contract, never turn invalid source into verified signal.
TIER RESEARCH_ONLY: Wyckoff inferred institutional volume claims, subjective Elliott/Harmonics, unquantified ICT/SMC, provider-unbacked Footprint/Delta/DOM, untuned ML and COT/CME context without access/rights. Monte Carlo is risk/simulation utility, not technical entry signal.
13.2 FEATURE CONTRACTS
Each feature must state: feature_id/code_version; hypothesis and exact computation; data inputs and quote price_side; bar/calendar source; as_of cutoff and first_known; OHLC volume semantics; lookback/warmup/session region; feature scope (zone, regime, trigger, risk, display); required vs optional; degradation when source absent; indicator-family overlap; experiment_manifest/ablation evidence and validity regime.
Causality: no future pivot/right bars, weekly COT release, indicator recalculation or subsequent source corrections before their first-known time. Daily/weekly candle boundaries must be provider-defined and DST-aware.
Quantity: nominal ATR/EMA/RSI parameter seeds are research candidates, not Forex-approved thresholds. No live/confirmed signal eligibility from arbitrary thresholds.
13.3 RECOMMENDED FIRST TESTABLE BASELINE
Structure first: D1/H4/H1 market structure and M15 trigger; conditional M5 verified micro confirmation; previous day/session high-low. Test EMA20/50 for trend role, ATR14 for scale, ADX14 OR RSI14 for supplementary regime/momentum. Add news/session and spread-as-ATR trade safety independently. Compare simple structure-only against addition of each indicator, then multiple combined using strict walk-forward and after-cost metrics. Don't build giant mandatory indicator stack.
13.4 QUALITY OF A TECHNICAL SIGNAL
A signal may be setup-detected but trade-ineligible if spread, bid-ask, macro event, low-liquidity period, margin or source quality blocks execution. Separate detecting a good technical pattern from recommending an executable trade.
13.5 ACCEPTANCE
FX-TA-01..FX-TA-10 are TEST-ID PLACEHOLDERS under 06 §5 REQ-FX-005; 01 §10 is the design decision record (there is no 01 §10.6). Detailed executable testcase definitions and evidence remain UNREGISTERED/NOT_RUN; source validation, broker costs, OOS and ablation still OPEN. User has requested list of KEEP/ADAPT/REMOVE/ADD throughout implementation; record every decision/change evidence in 01 §10. 
14. APPROVED_TA_SCOPE_20261106 — QUYẾT ĐỊNH PHẠM VI PHÂN TÍCH KỸ THUẬT
Ngày 06/11/2026: Người dùng phê duyệt các đề xuất loại bỏ và bổ sung phương pháp, công cụ, chỉ báo cho Forex. Quyết định này nâng phạm vi phương pháp từ DRAFT lên DESIGN_SCOPE_APPROVED; không nâng tham số, từng detector, dữ liệu, code hoặc hiệu quả giao dịch lên APPROVED/PASS.
CORE: Dow Theory + Price Action + Market Structure + S/R, multi-TF, ATR/volatility và bộ lọc động lượng/xu hướng có vai trò riêng.
REMOVE: VN-Index/sector relative strength và equity market breadth; tập trung volume toàn thị trường giả định từ tick volume broker; indicator consensus trùng lặp; mặc định lịch nến chứng khoán Việt Nam.
CONDITIONAL: VSA/VCP dựa volume, OBV, MFI, RVOL, VWAP/AVWAP/Volume Profile và order flow yêu cầu nguồn phù hợp, được cấp quyền; không chứng minh được thì không làm hard gate.
ADD: session high/low và previous day/week high/low, liquidity sweep, ADR, spread/ATR, volatility regime, currency strength, cross-pair correlation. Donchian, efficiency ratio/choppiness, CME futures/COT là optional/research cần kiểm chứng thêm.
BASELINE CANDIDATE: D1/H4/H1 structure, M15 early watch, M5 conditional; EMA20/50, ATR14, ADX14 hoặc RSI14 chỉ là ứng viên tham số. Khi build phải dùng cùng bộ feature contract trong Signal Engine và Backtest, có test nonlookahead, fee-aware OOS ablations. SPECIFIED / NOT_RUN.
CANONICAL CHANGE REGISTER: 01_FOREX_MIGRATION_REGISTER §10. NEXT: detector-by-detector dependency and validity manifest B1–B8/S1–S8.
15. HISTORICAL DETECTOR MANIFEST AUDIT v0.2 — ĐỀ XUẤT TRƯỚC DUYỆT, KHÔNG EXECUTABLE
Người dùng đồng ý rà soát sâu bộ lõi và 16 vùng B1–B8/S1–S8. Tài liệu chuẩn chi tiết: 03_FOREX_DETECTOR_AUDIT — 16 B/S Manifest & Gap Register v0.2, https://docs.google.com/document/d/1SBdP0a5PUvz8Y1nGPAlL75nHyDwuEJd-T-4uHDa0e2c/edit
R0 context, R_C candidate/watch, R_E early with CLOSED verified M15 event, R_F confirmed with applicable higher-TF causal structure, invalidation, required/optional/source feature, negative/no-lookahead tests MUST be specified per detector before implementation. Do not borrow Swing Trading 1.8 numeric thresholds/volume gates.
Allowed directions in DRAFT review: B1–B6,B8 LONG_CANDIDATE; B7 WATCH_ONLY; S1,S2,S4–S7 SHORT_CANDIDATE; S3 EXIT_LONG_ONLY; S8 WARNING_EXIT_LONG_ONLY. All entry candidates remain ACTION_ELIGIBLE=FALSE until provider/clock/risk/execution and detector test gates pass. If no user position, S3/S8 may still show a warning without inventing exit orders.
HISTORICAL PROPOSAL AT §15 (SUPERSEDED BY §16): FX-N01 bearish compression breakout and FX-N02 bearish momentum breakdown were pending user review at that time, to address stock-derived S taxonomy asymmetry without hijacking S3/S8. Not approved as rule IDs in live catalog.
Universal source and risk: distinguish quote price-side BID/ASK/MID, broker product, DST/week/end-of-day as-of, real costs/margin, macro timestamp; any required feature missing => UNVERIFIED. No provider-global volume, no hindsight pivot, no confirmed bar from provisional candle, no duplicate opportunities from overlapping detector evidence.
First quantification trials: B2/B5 LONG and S2/S7 SHORT. Test one executable rule at a time when licensed historical data and bidask cost/fill simulator are verified; label metrics event false alerts and net-R OOS separately. ALL detector tests and Backtest = SPECIFIED/NOT_RUN as of 06/11/2026.
16. FOREX DIRECTIONAL SETUP TAXONOMY v0.3 — EXTENSIBLE RULE REGISTRY
06/11/2026. User approved new SHORT zones FX-S9 and FX-S10 in DESIGN SCOPE only; earlier pending labels in §15 are superseded. Do not make them trade-eligible until concrete formula/fixtures and validated bid-ask data.
CURRENT CATALOG LABELS=18 (8 B legacy +8 S legacy +2 FX-S). ENTRY CANDIDATE IDs=15 (7 LONG, 8 SHORT), B7 watch-only=1, S3/S8 protective exit-only=2. Rule registry/coverage/audit iteration MUST be versioned dynamic; never hard-code 16 zones/8 S labels for the FOREX project.
Core analysis has eight family mechanisms for comparison: REVERSAL_MAJOR, TREND_PULLBACK, RANGE_REJECTION, FIRST_HL_LH, BREAK_RETEST, COMPRESSION_BREAK, MOMENTUM_BREAK, FAILED_BREAK_LIQUIDITY_SWEEP; each can have LONG/SHORT directional variant, but these 16 conceptual cells aren't '16 validated trades'. Overlap sharing origin_event_id emits one deduped opportunity. B3 long failed-breakdown variant within range is candidate; outside range FX-B9 remains research proposed, NOT added.
FUTURE decisions can MERGE/SPLIT/RETIRE/ADD only with exact causal predicate distinction, outcome data, standalone source availability, net OOS R and false-alert/lead-time improvement; no numeric symmetry quota. Session levels, Carry/Value/FX Fixing, currency strength, news filters may be strategies/variants/context and should not arbitrarily inflate zone counts.
FX-S9 SHORT compression breakdown spec: H1/H4 bearish context, causal lower compression boundary, M15 verified close break, H1/H4 confirmation, no VSA hard gate, bidask/ADR/spread/risk invalidation; overlaps S7/FX-S10. FX-S10 SHORT momentum breakdown spec: causal support, M15 close breakdown, H1/H4 confirmed extension, ADR/spread/news filter, no equity benchmark/RVOL mandatory; overlaps FX-S9/S7. Full manifest in Forex 03 §I.
Trade simulator caveat: actual spot FX has separate value-date settlement conventions, often T+2 for many currency pairs, not stock ownership T+2; retail rolling spot/CFD margin+rollover differs. Instrument-kind contract must distinguish spot/reference/CFD; no blanket 'Forex has no T+2'.

17. APPROVED FOUR-STAGE SIGNAL SEMANTICS — 06/11/2026
Hướng
Trạng thái hiển thị
Ý nghĩa thiết kế / ranh giới hành động
LONG
Chờ mua sớm
WAIT_EARLY — nhận biết sớm vùng đang hình thành; chưa phải điểm vào lệnh
LONG
Chờ mua
WAIT — theo dõi vùng đủ điều kiện bối cảnh; chờ trigger
LONG
Mua sớm
EARLY_SIGNAL — bằng chứng sớm từ nến đã xác minh; không mặc định đủ điều kiện khớp
LONG
Mua xác nhận
CONFIRMED_SIGNAL — mô hình kỹ thuật xác nhận; eligibility nguồn/chi phí/rủi ro vẫn tách riêng
SHORT
Chờ bán sớm
WAIT_EARLY — theo dõi vùng SHORT hình thành; không mang nghĩa thoát LONG
SHORT
Chờ bán
WAIT — vùng SHORT có bối cảnh nhưng chờ trigger
SHORT
Bán sớm
EARLY_SIGNAL — dấu hiệu SHORT sớm; không phải lệnh bán cổ phiếu
SHORT
Bán xác nhận
CONFIRMED_SIGNAL — mô hình SHORT đã xác nhận; chỉ có thể trade khi các gate riêng đạt
User approved retention of four main signal-display states PER ENTRY-ELIGIBLE DIRECTION, with separate protective/warning semantics for watch-only/exit-only zones. DESIGN_SCOPE_APPROVED, NOT_ENGINEERING_OR_BACKTEST_PASSED.
Same conceptual signal phases for 15 entry candidate IDs: B1–B6,B8 (LONG 7); S1,S2,S4,S5,S6,S7,FX-S9,FX-S10 (SHORT 8). These 15 are DESIGN CANDIDATES, not empirically eligible trade recommendations.
Formal model keeps orthogonal dimensions (a) direction LONG/SHORT, (b) candidate phase prewatch/approaching/early/confirmed, (c) action ENTRY_LONG/ENTRY_SHORT/EXIT_LONG/EXIT_SHORT/WATCH_ONLY, (d) execution eligibility, (e) data/source quality and risk gating. GUI labels are a rendering of facts, not an order and not enough to create action eligible status. No universal assumption that numeric/rank priority is strictly chronological or that every candidate transits through all four states; transitions can skip with causal evidence. Phase names alone do not imply equal criteria for all detector families; each rule's R0/RC/RE/RF manifests apply.
Meaningful guard: CHỜ ... SỚM = structurally prewatch ahead of eligible entry zone with defined proximity conditions; CHỜ ... = WATCH zone with required context; ... SỚM = conditional early evidence tied to closed verified lower-timeframe trigger and usable stop candidate, but not declared higher-timeframe confirmation; ... XÁC NHẬN = all rule-defined confirmed predicates, appropriate closed source candles, invalidation, entry/SL/TP, net after-cost risk feasibility and audits satisfied. Numeric thresholds, timing and exact grade boundaries remain PENDING calibration.
B7 = WATCH_ONLY_DEFAULT even if early bullish micro evidence appears; cannot emit MUA SỚM/MUA XÁC NHẬN as trade recommendation until its special countertrend strategy is separately approved and verified. S3/S8 = protective warning/EXIT_LONG_ONLY; may expose WATCH/EARLY/CONFIRMED WARNING progression, never BÁN SỚM/BÁN XÁC NHẬN meaning opening a new SHORT. With no verified LONG position, show warning only; no calculated actual exit size/order.
Side status separate: INVALIDATED, NO_TRADE, UNVERIFIED, ERROR; data missing => UNVERIFIED (not false NOT_DETECTED); technical setup detected but blocked by spread/news/margin/etc => SETUP_DETECTED, TRADE_INELIGIBLE/NO_TRADE. Conflicting long/short for same pair => conflict policy/no double order, preserve evidence. Provisional/open candles cannot produce CONFIRMED; M5 conditional trigger not override D1/H4 confirm; first_known timestamps and no-lookahead; idempotent rescan same closed bar.
Acceptance tests FX-STATE-01..12 SPECIFIED/NOT_RUN: LONG 4 labels, SHORT 4 labels, skipped-state causal transitions, early-vs-confirmed no-lookahead, M5-only false confirmation negative, B7 watch-only, S3/S8 protective-only, absent position warning-only, missing data UNVERIFIED, spread/news blocked NO_TRADE, duplicate bar idempotence, same event dual side conflict.
This decision SUPERCEDES earlier ambiguity in §2 SIGNAL_STATUS enum and §11 UI proposal only to the extent of these four approved display labels; technical enums may keep different names with explicit mapping. No separate user approval to lock Forex visual UI design or trigger parameters.

MANDATORY PRINCIPLES: independent private versioned GitHub code repository planned, separate Drive source-of-truth specs and Handoff; typed modular Python contracts/adapters/feature/structure/zone/state/risk/audit/cache/report; common deterministic domain library for live analysis and historical Backtest; immutable source revisions/as-of/first-known; fail-closed for required data/quote/calendar/risk; meaningful unit, integration, contract, regression, negative, replay, quote-side, DST and OOS tests; PR/CI review, test evidence and versioned decisions; selective incremental caching and provenance; no auto broker trading.
RELEASE STAGES are independently tracked: DOCS APPROVED != CODE CI PASS != SOURCE QUALIFIED != DETECTOR TESTS PASS != HISTORICAL BACKTEST != OOS != PAPER != LIVE. Do not inherit equity project Python CI results or tests as Forex proof. Dynamic Forex rule registry currently 18 catalog labels/15 entry design candidates, rule-specific four display states, zero executable validated detectors.

Do not wait for all rules to begin measuring one rule. No unapproved production broker actions/deploy.

19. INDEPENDENT TECHNICAL CORE AUDIT FINDINGS — GAP REGISTER, NOT APPROVED EXECUTABLE RULES
06/11/2026. Latest audit found gaps in otherwise coherent modular architecture. This §19 documents missing contracts; it does NOT approve thresholds, add trade-entry zones or claim tests.
GAP-CORE-01 P0 EXIT_SHORT PROTECTION: Step 04 Position & Exit Protection is direction-neutral in words but lacks specific SHORT position risk lifecycle, stop/cover trigger side, trailing stops, partial close/margin/rollover and sequence/position verification fixtures. S3/S8 currently protect LONG only; B7 WATCH_ONLY is not automatically an EXIT_SHORT order. Separate protection from 18 zone catalog (no extra entry-zone count).
GAP-CORE-02 P0 EXECUTION BAR PATH: Define broker-specific quote-side activation and fill of SL/TP/stop/limit, simultaneous SL+TP inside candle, spread gap, partial fill, swap rollover, margin stopout, inverse/base/quote/account conversions, and staleness/first-known; never choose optimistic fills by assumption.
GAP-CORE-03 P0 FOUR-STATE FORMULA: Define deterministic predicates/thresholds and evidence per detector for prewatch/wait/early/confirmed; clarify technical_pattern_phase separately from TRADE_ELIGIBILITY and from REPORT_DISPLAY_STATE; expiration/rearm, skip, conflict, invalidation, replay and repeated-bar protection must be tested. Keep user-approved Vietnamese labels and B7/S3/S8 exceptions.
GAP-CORE-04 P0 RULE TESTABILITY: 16 legacy and two new detector manifests remain qualitative. Pin feature_id, temporal event/as-of semantics, quote side, prerequisite source quality, normalized ATR units, lookback/warmup, invalidation and golden/negative source fixtures before any actionable flag.
GAP-CORE-05 P1 REGISTRY/CACHE: Versioned contract/event IDs, deterministic dedup and overlap priority, L1–L5 invalidation/restore, full vs incremental parity, scanner concurrency and bounded resource usage need executable specifications and benchmarks.
GAP-CORE-06 P0 DATA EVIDENCE: Instrument/broker/product, permission, historical BID/ASK (not MID only), spread/slippage and news/calendar validation; no real market backtest with unspecified provider.
GAP-CORE-07 P1 NONFUNCTIONAL: API/error reason codes, observability/SLO, UI data DTO/UAT, retention/security/backup and operational release controls not validated.
RISK-OF-CONFUSION FIXED: §2 reference to 16 is now expressly historical; §13.5 incorrect nonexistent Migration Register §10.6 citation corrected. FX-TA-01..10 are TEST-ID PLACEHOLDERS, not runnable tests. §15 preapproval FX-N01/02 is historical; latest §16 FX-S9/FX-S10 design-scope approval prevails.
REFERENCE: QA full project report 06 §10 https://docs.google.com/document/d/19CGNHVw1QqnTifmtf1Xu5bst0IAbEWLhQlgOoVzfYZg/edit
